- **tiime-upload-justificatifs**: Use this skill to push the structured YAML fiches produced by `justificatif-describe` into Tiime via a Chromium piloted by Playwright MCP. The fiches live in `$WORKSPACE/receipts/YYYY-MM/`. The skill checks whether each piece already exists in Tiime (auto-created from Revolut sync), attaches the PDF/JPG if missing, and completes the metadata (date, total_incl_tax, vat, label, invoice no., intra-EU VAT number, SIREN). Never validates a piece on first run — stops for human confirmation. Trigger when the user says « upload tiime », « pousse les justificatifs dans Tiime », « complète Tiime avec l… ([GerardPaligot/accountant-toolkit](https://github.com/GerardPaligot/accountant-toolkit/tree/HEAD/plugins/accountant/skills/tiime-upload-justificatifs))

- **tiiny-host**: Publish files or pages made in the conversation as live websites on Tiiny Host, and manage the user's existing Tiiny Host sites.
